# Posterior Data Preparation

Generates the input matrix for the simulation of the cointegration
matrix \\\beta\\.

## Usage

``` r
coint_prepare_sur_data(w, alpha, k, r, reparameterise, tvp)
```

## Arguments

- w:

  a \\T \times M\\ matrix.

- alpha:

  a \\K \times r\\ matrix of the loading matrix \\\alpha\\.

- k:

  integer of the number of endogenous variables in the model.

- r:

  integer with the rank of the cointegration matrix \\\Pi\\.

- reparameterise:

  logical indicating if the reparameterisation described in Koop et
  al. (2010) should be performed.

- tvp:

  logical indicating if the SUR matrix with the values of regressors
  should be prepared for the estimation of constant or time varying
  parameters.

## Value

A sparse block matrix.

## Examples

``` r

data("e6") # Load data

# Generate model input
mod <- create_bvecmodel(e6, p = 4, r = 1,
                        const = "unrestricted", seasonal = "unrestricted")

# Obtain input data
alpha <- matrix(c(-0.1, 0.16, -0.04, -0.02), 2)
w <- mod$data$train$w

# Constant coefficients
coint_prepare_sur_data(w, alpha, 2, 1, TRUE, FALSE)
```
